Let's design a robust local optimizer in detail.

Function softmax(v):
  v = v - max(v); e=exp(np.clip(v,-745,700)); s=e.sum(); return e/s.

Function compute_b(a,m): F=fft(a,m); b=ifft(F*F).real[:L]; return np.maximum(b,0)

Function weighted_grad(a,w,m): F=fft(a,m); P=fft(w,m); c=ifft(P*np.conj(F)).real[:n]; return c (this computes ∑ a_j w_{i+j}? Need verify with signed and zero-padding. Previous used this. Let's test with simple n=2, a=[a0,a1], b0=a0^2, b1=2a0a1,b2=a1^2. If w=[w0,w1,w2], gradient w.r.t a0 of ∑w_k b_k = 2(a0 w0 + a1 w1). Their c_i =∑_j a_j w_{i+j}. For i=0: a0 w0 + a1 w1. Good (times2). i=1: a0 w1 + a1 w2. Good. So c=∂/2. Great.

Function smooth_weights(b, tau_frac): M=b.max(); tau=tau_frac*M; if tau<=0 return top uniform; x=(b-M)/tau; x-=max; w=exp(clip(x,-50,50)); if sum small, w top; normalize.

Variance weights: wv=(b - mean); norm=sum(abs(wv)); if norm>0 wv/=norm.

Combined weights: w = wmax + var_weight * wv. But wmax sum1, wv sumabs1. Could make negative. Need ensure not all zero. If var_weight large, gradient maybe noisy. We can also use high-only variance: wv_pos = np.maximum(b - mean,0); normalize; nonnegative. This pushes high down and doesn't actively raise low, but max gradient already. Signed can raise low beneficial. Use signed with limited magnitude 0.5.

Adam update:
  G = a*(c - np.dot(c,a))  # gradient wrt v for ∑w b? Factor 2 omitted
  G = G - mean(G) (should zero)
  adam stats, lr schedule.
  v += lr * step? We are minimizing, so v -= lr*step? Previous did v = v - lr*step where step=mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps) with G as gradient. We'll do same.
  center v, clip.

We can also use "score-based annealing": track best_score. If current_score significantly worse than best, reset v to best v and add small noise. This prevents drift.

Pseudo local_search:
def local_search(a0, seconds, seed, verbose=False):
  rng=default_rng(seed)
  v=log(a0); center; clip
  m=next_pow2(2n-1)
  best_a=a0; best_sc=score
  var_state zeros; t=0; lr_factor=1; no_improve=0
  start=time; deadline=start+seconds
  check_interval = max(1, min(10, 2000//n?)) maybe 2.
  while time<deadline:
    progress=(time-start)/seconds
    tau_frac = 0.25*(1-progress)+0.005  # from .25 to .005
    var_w = 0.6*(1-progress) # from .6 to0? or keep .2
    sigma = 0.05
    v,a,b=...
    # compute current score
    sc = true_score(b)
    if sc<best_sc: best... no_improve=0 else no_improve += check
    if no_improve > stuck_limit: v = v_best + noise; boost lr; continue
    wmax = soft weights
    wv = signed variance normalized
    w = wmax + var_w*wv
    c=weighted_grad
    G=a*(c-dot(c,a))
    # optional adjust learning rate by gradient norm
    adam update, step clip
    v_update = v - lr*step
    v_update -= mean; clip
    # optional candidate score every line_search_freq (e.g. 5)
    if t % 5 ==0:
       a_new=softmax(v_update); b_new=...; sc_new=score
       if sc_new < sc: v=v_update accept
       else: # revert, reduce lr? maybe if no accept 5 times reduce tau? 
    else: v=v_update
  return best

Let's design weights function:
def make_weights(b, tau_frac, rho_top, top_frac):
  M = b.max(); L=b.size
  tau = max(1e-12, tau_frac*M)
  x=(b-M)/tau; x-=x.max(); e=np.exp(clip(x,-60,60)); sw=e.sum(); if sw>0: w=e/sw else: uniform
  K = max(1, int(L*top_frac)); idx=argpartition(b,-K)[:K]? top K. wtop=np.zeros(L); wtop[idx]=1/K
  w = (1-rho_top)*w + rho_top*wtop
  return w

def _weighted_conv(a,w,m,n):
   F = np.fft.fft(a,m)  # could reuse F from b if passed
   P = np.fft.fft(w,m)

Softmax:
```
def _softmax(v):
   v = np.asarray(v, float)
   v = v - v.max()
   e = np.exp(v)
   s = e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return ones/n
   return e/s
```
Since v centered and clipped, no overflow.

def _make_inits(n, rng, best_a, max_count=10):
    inits=[]
    if best_a is not None: inits.append(_interp(best_a,n))
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    i=np.arange(n)
    # uniform
    inits.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    # simple shapes
    for eps in (0.2,0.4,0.6,0.85):
        a=1+eps*np.cos(np.pi*t)
        inits.append(_normalize_nonneg(a))
        a=1+eps*np.cos(np.pi*(t-0.5))
        inits.append(...)
    # powers
    for p in (0.3,0.5,1.0,2.0):
        a=(np.maximum(t,1e-6)*np.maximum(1-t,1e-6))**p
        inits.append(normalize)
    # gaussians
    for _ in range(4):
       k=int(rng.integers(2,6)); centers=rng.uniform(0.1,0.9,k); sigmas=rng.uniform(0.05,0.25,k); amps=rng.uniform(0.5,1.5,k); a=sum exp(-((t-c)/s)^2)
    # random fourier
    for K, decay, base in [(8,0.8,0.8),(16,0.7,0.7),(32,0.6,0.6),(64,0.5,0.5)]:
       a=base
       for k in 1..K:
          amp=(1-decay? no) maybe 0.6/(k**0.6)
          ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
          freq = k  # 2π k
          a += amp*np.cos(2π*k*t + ph)
       # maybe also cos(kπt) low modes:
       a += 0.5*np.cos(π*t)
       inits.append(...)
    # spectral from best if not None: target flat mix current b? Skip or simple random phase flat:
    return diverse selection
```

def _select_inits(inits, max_count):
   unique=[]; scores=[]
   for a in inits:
      a=_normalize_nonneg(a); sc=score_fft(a)
      if not finite: continue
      diverse=True

         d=np.linalg.norm(a-b)
         if d<0.05: diverse=False; break
      if diverse: unique.append(a); scores.append(sc)
      if len(unique)>=max_count*2? break
   sort by sc; keep top max_count maybe also ensure diversity.

def _score_b(a,m,n):
    a=np.asarray(float); S=float(a.sum())
    if S<=0: return inf,None
    aa=a/S  # for FFT? Can't scale? Since score invariant, using normalized to avoid large. Better normalize.
    F=fft(aa,m); b=ifft(F*F).real[:L]; b=np.maximum(b,0); bmax=b.max(); return 2*n*bmax, b
```

Pseudo code more concrete:

```
def _local_search(a0, n, seconds, seed, verbose=False):
    if seconds <= 0.5:
        a=_normalize_nonneg(a0); sc,_=_score_b(a,m,n); return a,sc
    rng = default_rng(seed)
    n=int(n); L=2*n-1; m=_next_pow2(L)
    a=_normalize_nonneg(a0)
    v=_log_init(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_v=v.copy(); best_sc,_=_score_b(a,m,n)
    cur_v=v.copy()
    mu=np.zeros(n); nu=np.zeros(n); t=0
    lr=0.35; fail=0; last_best=time.monotonic()
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(1.0,seconds)
    accept_tol=1e-10
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if time.monotonic() - last_best > min(90.0, 0.25*seconds + 10.0):
            prog=...
            sigma = 0.15 + 0.45*(1.0-prog)
            cur_v = best_v + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            _center_clip(cur_v)
            lr = 0.25
            fail = 0
            last_best = time.monotonic()
            continue
        prog = min(1.0,(time.monotonic()-start)/max(1.0,seconds))
        a=_softmax(cur_v)
        F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
        b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:L]
        b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
        sc=float(2*n*b.max())  # a sum1
        if not finite: break
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); best_v=cur_v.copy(); last_best=...; fail=0
        M=float(b.max())
        if M<=0: break
        tau_frac = 0.4 * (0.02/0.4)**prog + 0.02
        tau = max(1e-15, tau_frac*M)
        top_frac = 0.05*(0.003/0.05)**prog + 0.003
        rho_top = 0.1 + 0.6*prog
        alpha_sq = 0.35*(1.0-prog) + 0.05
        # lse current
        x=(b-M)/tau; x-=x.max(); expx=np.exp(np.clip(x,-60,60)); sw=float(expx.sum());
        if sw>0: wmax=expx/sw else wmax=np.full(L,1/L)
        K=max(1,int(L*top_frac)); idx=np.argpartition(b,-K)[-K:]; wtop=np.zeros(L); wtop[idx]=1.0/K
        bsum=float(b.sum()); wsq = b/bsum if bsum>0 else wmax
        # optional signed
        if rng.random() < 0.15 and prog < 0.8:
            mean=bmean? = bsum/L
            wv=b-mean
            sv=float(np.sum(np.abs(wv)))
            if sv>0: wv= wv/sv
            w = 0.6*wmax + 0.2*wtop + 0.2*wq? + 0.2*wv ??
        else:
            total = (1-rho_top-alpha_sq) + rho_top + alpha_sq
            w = ((1-rho_top-alpha_sq)*wmax + rho_top*wtop + alpha_sq*wsq)/total
        # If negative, maybe scale
        if np.any(w < -0.2): w = np.maximum(w, -0.2)
        # Maybe renormalize positive? Not necessary.
        P=np.fft.fft(w,m)
        c=np.fft.ifft(P*np.conj(F)).real[:n]
        G = a*(c - float(np.dot(c,a)))
        G = G - float(np.mean(G))
        gn=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(G*G)))
        if not finite or gn<1e-14:
            cur_v += rng.normal(0,0.05,n); center; continue
        t+=1
        mu=0.9*mu+0.1*G
        nu=0.999*nu+0.001*G*G
        mhat=mu/(1.0-0.9**t)
        vhat=nu/(1.0-0.999**t)
        step=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
        step=np.clip(step,-1.0,1.0)
        # if step sum not zero, center? Step from G centered but Adam may not; center step? Since v translation invariance, center step too.
        step = step - np.mean(step)
        srms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(step*step)))
        if srms < 1e-12:
            continue
        # Maybe divide step by srms and adjust lr? If lr=0.35 and srms~0.1, small. Could do step = step/srms * max(0.05, min(1.0,srms))? Simpler: use step as is, lr*step.
        base=lr
        best_cand_v=None; best_cand_sc=sc; best_cand_lse=None
        accepted=False
        for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            v_try=cur_v - base*fac*step
            _center_clip(v_try)
            a_try=_softmax(v_try)
            F2=np.fft.fft(a_try,m)
            b2=np.fft.ifft(F2*F2).real[:L]; b2=np.maximum(b2,0.0)
            sc2=float(2*n*b2.max())
            # compute lse2 maybe
            M2=float(b2.max())
            x2=(b2-M2)/tau; x2-=x2.max(); s2=float(np.exp(np.clip(x2,-60,60)).sum())
            lse2 = M2 + (np.log(s2/L) if s2>0 else 0.0)
            # current lse:
            # we haven't computed lse_cur. compute now from b (reuse sw): lse_cur = M + log(sw/L)
            if sc2 < sc - 1e-10:
                 accept; break
            # track best candidate by sc then lse
            if sc2 < best_cand_sc - 1e-10 or (abs(sc2-best_cand_sc)<=1e-10 and lse2<best_cand_lse...): update
        if not accepted:
            # check best candidate with lse improvement
            if best_cand_sc < sc + max(1e-8, 1e-6*best_sc) and best_cand_lse < lse_cur - 1e-5:
                 accept best_cand
        if accepted:
            cur_v=...
            # update current sc? We may have sc from accepted. If accepted from raw, sc2. Need recompute? We have b2? We can set current_sc? But next loop recomputes. For acceptance state, no need.
            if accepted_sc < best_sc: update
            fail=0
            if accepted_sc < sc: lr=min(lr*1.05, 1.2)
            else: lr=max(lr*0.95,0.01)
        else:
            fail+=1
            lr=max(lr*0.85,0.005)
            if fail > 80:
                # noise reset
                cur_v = best_v + rng.normal(0, 0.1+0.3*prog, n); center; fail=0; lr=0.25
```

def _process_stage(n, budget, best_a, best_score, rng):
   if budget<20: just interpolate and score
   m=next_pow2(2n-1)
   inits = _make_inits(n,rng,best_a)
   # quick score top
   cand=[]
   for a in inits:
      a=_normalize; sc,_=_score_b(a,m,n)
      cand.append((sc,a))
   # diversity
   selected=[]
   for sc,a in sorted(cand):
      diverse=True
      for b in selected:
         if np.linalg.norm(a-b)<0.04: diverse=False; break
      if diverse: selected.append((sc,a))
      if len(selected)>=8: break
   # allocate time
   weights=[0.55,0.25,0.15,0.05,0.0,0.0,...]
   for rank,(sc,a) in enumerate(selected[:4]):
      alloc = max(10, budget*weight[rank])
      if time... break
      a_opt, sc_opt = _local_search(a,n,alloc, seed)
      update
   # if remaining > 30, run on best_a at this n for remaining
   rem=budget - elapsed
   if rem>30: local_search(best_a_interp, n, rem, seed)
   return best_a,best_score

Detailed LBFGS:
```
def _lbfgs_polish(a0, n, seconds, seed=0, p=24):
    try: from scipy.optimize import minimize
    except: return a0, score
    import numpy as np
    a=np.asarray; normalize
    L=2n-1; m=next_pow2(L)
    nvar=n-1
    # initial y from v=log(a)-log(a[0])? Since xfull[0]=0, v_i=log(a_i)-log(a_0). But a_0 may be zero. Use v=log(max(a,eps)); v-=v[0]
    v0=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); y0=v0[1:]-v0[0]
    def fun_and_jac(y, return_jac=True):
        v=np.empty(n); v[0]=0; v[1:]=y
        # center? v0 fixed, no need. But v can large; softmax.
        a=_softmax(v)
        F=fft(a,m); b=ifft(F*F).real[:L]; b=np.maximum(b,0)
        # for power norm use logsumexp(p log b) / p + log? We can add small eps
        logb=np.log(np.maximum(b,1e-300))
        z=p*logb; z-=z.max(); s=np.exp(z).sum(); if s<=0: val=p*? ; grad zeros
        val=(z.max()+np.log(s/p? wait logsumexp(z))/p)??? logsumexp(z)=zmax+log(s). J=(zmax+log(s))/p.
        weights = exp((z-zmax)) / sum? w=b^(p-1) normalized.
        P=fft(w,m); c=ifft(P*conj(F)).real[:n]
        Gv=a*(c-dot(c,a))
        if return_jac: return Gv[1:]
        return val
    def f(y): return fun_and_jac(y,False)
    def jac(y): return fun_and_jac(y,True)
    callback time flag.
    res=minimize(f,y0,jac=jac,method='L-BFGS-B',options={'maxiter':500,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-7,'maxcor':20,'disp':False},callback=cb)
    From res.x get v, a, score. Keep if better.
```

Let's write code with helper `_eval_candidate(a, m, n, tau)` to avoid repetition. It will compute b2, score, lse. But called many times; function call overhead okay. Could inline in local for speed? Use helper for clarity. Since no closures, top-level.

```
def _eval_candidate(a, m, n, tau):
    F = np.fft.fft(a,m)
    b = np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]
    b = np.maximum(b,0.0)
    bsum=float(b.sum())
    bmax=float(b.max())
    if bsum<=0 or not finite: return np.inf, np.inf
    score = 2.0*n*bmax/(bsum*bsum)
    if tau <= 1e-30:
        return score, bmax
    x=(b-bmax)/tau
    x -= float(x.max())
    x = np.clip(x, -60.0, 0.0)
    s=float(np.exp(x).sum())
    if s<=0: lse=bmax
    else: lse = bmax + float(np.log(s / b.size))
    return score, lse
```
For current, we compute F,b and score/lse similarly. Could use helper but need F,c. Fine.

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _next_pow2(x):
    x=int(max(1,x))
    p=1
    while p<x: p<<=1
    return p

def _normalize_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0:
        return np.ones(1)/1.0
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size,1.0/a.size)
    return a/s

def _interp(a,n): ...
```

```
def _init_log(a):
    a=_normalize_nonneg(a)
    v=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    v=v-v.mean()
    return np.clip(v,-30,30)
```

`_center_clip(v)`:
```
def _center_clip(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v -= float(np.mean(v))
    np.clip(v, -30.0, 30.0, out=v)
    return v
```
But if called with v already array, returns same. In local, do `cur_v = _center_clip(cur_v + noise)` etc. If function creates copy? `np.asarray` may return view; okay. To avoid modifying input unexpectedly, not issue.

`_softmax(v)`:
```
def _softmax(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    v=v-v.max()
    e=np.exp(v)
    s=float(e.sum())
    n=v.size
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    return e/s
```
Since v clipped, no overflow. But if v all very negative? center ensures max 0.

`_score_b(a,m,n)`:
```
def _score_b(a,m,n):
    a=_normalize_nonneg(a)
    L=2*n-1
    F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
    b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:L]
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    bmax=float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(bmax) or bmax<=0: return np.inf, b
    return float(2.0*n*bmax), b
```
Since normalized sum1, score =2n bmax. If we normalized inside, yes. If input unnormalized, normalize. Good.

No lambda! Important. In Python, `sorted(lst, key=...)` with key a function reference not lambda okay (e.g. `key=itemgetter(0)` from operator). We can import `operator.itemgetter`. That's fine. Or sort tuples `(sc, idx)` because first elements floats distinct. Use import operator.

Diversity:
```
def _select_diverse(scored_arrays, max_keep, thresh=0.055):
    # scored_arrays: list of (sc, a)
    scored_arrays.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    out=[]
    for sc,a in scored_arrays:
       if not np.isfinite(sc): continue
       diverse=True
       for b in out:
           d=np.sqrt(np.dot(a-b,a-b))
           if d < thresh:
               diverse=False; break
       if diverse:
           out.append((sc,a))
           if len(out)>=max_keep: break
    return out
```
But if a good one is duplicate, later diverse maybe lower score. To fill max_keep if few diverse, we can after loop if len<max_keep, add remaining sorted regardless of diversity. Good.

`_process_stage`:
```
def _process_stage(n, stage_deadline, best_a, best_score, rng):
    now=time.monotonic()
    if now >= stage_deadline: return best_a,best_score
    n=int(n)
    L=2*n-1; m=_next_pow2(L)
    # quick budget
    if stage_deadline - now < 25:
        if best_a is not None:
            a=_interp(best_a,n); sc,_=_score_b(a,m,n)
            return (a,sc) if sc<best_score else (best_a,best_score)
        a=np.ones(n)/n; sc,_=_score_b(a,m,n); return a,sc
    inits=_make_inits(n,rng,best_a)
    scored=[]
    for a in inits:
       a=_normalize_nonneg(a)
       sc,_=_score_b(a,m,n)
       if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,a))
    sel=_select_diverse(scored, 6, thresh=0.055)
    # If best_a interp not in top? ensure included? It likely is. But maybe not. We can prepend if not close? 
    # allocate time
    weights=(0.68,0.20,0.08,0.04)
    elapsed0=time.monotonic()
    stage_time_max=stage_deadline-elapsed0
    for rank,(sc,a) in enumerate(sel[:4]):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now + 20 >= stage_deadline: break
       wgt=weights[rank] if rank < len(weights) else 0.02
       alloc=min(wgt*stage_time_max, stage_deadline-now-10)
       if alloc < 12: alloc=max(5, stage_deadline-now-10)
       if alloc <=0: break
       seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31-1))
       a_opt, sc_opt=_local_search(a, n, alloc, seed, verbose=False)
       if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt<best_score:
           best_a=_normalize_nonneg(a_opt); best_score=float(sc_opt); print(...)
    # if remaining > 45, use best_a at this n for remaining with smaller seed
    now=time.monotonic()
    if now + 45 < stage_deadline:
       a_init = _interp(best_a, n) if best_a is not None else np.ones(n)/n
       seed=int(rng.integers(0,2**31-1))
       a_opt, sc_opt=_local_search(a_init, n, stage_deadline-now-5, seed)
       if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt<best_score: update
    return best_a,best_score
```

Random Fourier with phases: Use 2π frequencies and pi frequencies:
```
def _add_fourier(out,n,rng,K,use_pi=False,base=0.9):
   a=np.full(n, base)
   amp=0.55
   for k in range(1,K+1):
      if amp<0.02: break
      ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
      if use_pi: a += amp*np.cos(k*np.pi*t + ph)
      else: a += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t + ph)
      amp *= rng.uniform(0.5,0.75)
   out.append(_normalize_nonneg(a))
```

Let's write local search code carefully with variables.

```
def _local_search(a0, n, seconds, seed, verbose=False):
    n=int(n)
    seconds=float(seconds)
    if seconds <= 0:
       a=_normalize_nonneg(a0); sc,_=_score_b(a,m,n); return a,sc
    rng=np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    L=2*n-1; m=_next_pow2(L)
    a=_normalize_nonneg(a0)
    v=_init_log(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_v=v.copy(); best_sc,_=_score_b(a,m,n)
    cur_v=v.copy()
    mu=np.zeros(n); nu=np.zeros(n); t=0
    lr=0.35
    fail=0
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(1.0,seconds)
    last_best=start
    factor=2.0*float(n)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now - last_best > min(90.0, 0.25*seconds + 15.0):
           prog=min(1.0,(now-start)/max(1.0,seconds))
           sigma=0.12 + 0.40*(1.0-prog)
           cur_v = best_v + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
           cur_v = _center_clip(cur_v)
           lr=0.25
           fail=0
           last_best=now
           continue
       prog=...
       a=_softmax(cur_v)
       F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
       b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:L]
       b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
       bsum=float(b.sum())
       bmax=float(b.max())
       if bsum<=1e-300 or not np.isfinite(bmax): break
       sc=factor*bmax/(bsum*bsum)
       if not np.isfinite(sc): break
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); best_v=cur_v.copy(); last_best=now; fail=0
       if sc > best_sc + 0.01:
          cur_v=best_v.copy(); continue
       M=bmax
       tau_frac=0.40 * (0.02/0.40)**prog + 0.02
       tau_frac *= float(rng.uniform(0.75,1.3))
       tau=max(1e-15, tau_frac*M)
       top_frac=0.05 * (0.003/0.05)**prog + 0.003
       top_frac = max(1.0/float(L), min(0.2, top_frac))
       rho_top=0.1 + 0.6*prog
       alpha_sq=0.35*(1.0-prog)+0.05
       # weights
       x=(b-M)/tau
       x -= float(x.max())
       x=np.clip(x,-60.0,0.0)
       e=np.exp(x)
       sw=float(e.sum())
       if sw>0 and np.isfinite(sw): wmax=e/sw
       else: wmax=np.full(L,1.0/L)
       K=max(1,int(round(L*top_frac)))
       if K>=L: idx=np.arange(L)
       else: idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
       wtop=np.zeros(L); wtop[idx]=1.0/float(K)
       if bsum>0: wsq=b/bsum
       else: wsq=wmax
       use_signed = (rng.random() < (0.35 if prog<0.4 else (0.15 if prog<0.8 else 0.0)))
       if use_signed:
           mean=bsum/float(L)
           wv=b-mean
           sv=float(np.sum(np.abs(wv)))
           if sv>0: wv=wv/sv
           w = 0.5*wmax + 0.2*wtop + 0.2*wsq + 0.1*wv
       else:
           base=1.0-rho_top-alpha_sq
           if base < 0.0: base=0.0; # renorm
           w = (base*wmax + rho_top*wtop + alpha_sq*wsq)
           wsum=float(w.sum())
           if wsum>0: w=w/wsum
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)): w=np.full(L,1.0/L)
       P=np.fft.fft(w,m)
       c=np.fft.ifft(P*np.conj(F)).real[:n]
       G=a*(c - float(np.dot(c,a)))
       G = G - float(np.mean(G))
       gn=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(G*G)))
       if not np.isfinite(gn) or gn < 1e-14:
           cur_v = cur_v + rng.normal(0.0,0.03,n)
           cur_v=_center_clip(cur_v)
           continue
       t += 1
       mu=0.9*mu + 0.1*G
       nu=0.999*nu + 0.001*(G*G)
       mhat=mu/(1.0 - 0.9**t)
       vhat=nu/(1.0 - 0.999**t)
       step=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
       step=np.clip(step,-1.0,1.0)
       step = step - float(np.mean(step))
       srms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(step*step)))
       if not np.isfinite(srms) or srms <= 1e-14:
           continue
       # current lse
       lse_cur = M + float(np.log(sw / float(L))) if sw>0 else M
       best_cand_v=None; best_cand_sc=sc; best_cand_lse=lse_cur
       accepted=False; accepted_v=None; accepted_sc=sc
       for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
           v_try = cur_v - lr*fac*step
           v_try = _center_clip(v_try)
           a_try = _softmax(v_try)
           sc2,lse2 = _eval_candidate(a_try, m, n, tau)
           if not np.isfinite(sc2):
               continue
           if sc2 < best_cand_sc - 1e-12 or (abs(sc2-best_cand_sc) <= 1e-12 and lse2 < best_cand_lse - 1e-12):
               best_cand_sc=sc2; best_cand_lse=lse2; best_cand_v=v_try.copy()
           if sc2 < sc - 1e-10:
               accepted=True; accepted_v=v_try; accepted_sc=sc2; break
       if not accepted and best_cand_v is not None:
           tol_raw = max(1e-8, 1e-6*best_sc)
           if best_cand_sc < sc + tol_raw and best_cand_lse < lse_cur - 1e-6:
               accepted=True; accepted_v=best_cand_v; accepted_sc=best_cand_sc
       if accepted:
           cur_v = accepted_v
           if accepted_sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
               # need update best_a/v? We don't have a for accepted_v. Could compute quickly? We can set best_v=accepted_v; best_sc=accepted_sc; best_a maybe recompute if verbose.
               best_sc=accepted_sc
               best_v=accepted_v.copy()
               # best_a = _softmax(accepted_v) maybe expensive but okay for update.
               best_a=_softmax(accepted_v)
               last_best=now
           fail=0
           if accepted_sc < sc:
               lr = min(1.2, lr*1.04)
           else:
               lr = max(0.01, lr*0.98)
       else:
           fail += 1
           lr = max(0.005, lr*0.85)
           if fail > 60:
               sigma = 0.1 + 0.25*(1.0-prog)
               cur_v = best_v + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
               cur_v = _center_clip(cur_v)
               lr=0.2
               fail=0
    # final evaluate
    a_out=_softmax(best_v) if best_v is not None else best_a
    a_out=_normalize_nonneg(a_out)
    sc_out,_=_score_b(a_out,m,n)
    if not np.isfinite(sc_out) or sc_out>best_sc:
         a_out=best_a; sc_out=best_sc
    return a_out, float(sc_out)
```

Implement:
```
def _spectral_target_init(target, a_start, n, m, iters=30, blend=0.5):
    L=2*n-1
    target=np.asarray(target,float).copy()
    if target.size<L: pad; else truncate
    target=np.maximum(target,0)
    tm=target.max(); if tm>0: target/=tm else ones
    T=np.fft.fft(target,m)
    a=_normalize_nonneg(a_start)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
    tnorm=float(target.sum())? If target normalized max, sum maybe L/(2n-1)? Not important.
    for it in range(iters):
       F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
       eps=1e-12*(1+np.abs(F).max())? Scalar eps.
       Fnew=0.5*(F + T/(F+eps))
       anew=np.fft.ifft(Fnew).real[:n]
       pos=np.maximum(anew,0)
       ps=pos.sum()
       if ps<1e-8*max(a.sum(),1e-12): pos=a
       bld=_blend max(0.2, blend*(1-0.5*it/iters))
       a=(1-bld)*a + bld*pos
       a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
    a=_normalize_nonneg(a)
    return a
```
This is similar to previous but target fixed. It might produce positive sequences approximating target. Use target flat: ones/L. Also use target from best_a b normalized (if best_a not None) blended with flat. We can add one or two spectral inits per stage.

Let's write `_process_stage` robust:
```
def _process_stage(n, stage_deadline, best_a, best_score, rng):
    now=time.monotonic()
    n=int(n)
    if now >= stage_deadline or n<3: return best_a,best_score
    L=2*n-1; m=_next_pow2(L)
    if stage_deadline - now < 20:
        init = _interp(best_a,n) if best_a is not None else np.ones(n)/n
        sc,_=_score_b(init,m,n)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score: return _normalize_nonneg(init), sc
        return best_a,best_score
    inits=_make_inits(n,rng,best_a,m)
    scored=[]
    for a in inits:
       sc,_=_score_b(a,m,n)
       if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,a))
    if not scored:
       a=np.ones(n)/n; sc=2.0; scored=[(sc,a)]
    sel=_select_diverse(scored, 6, 0.055)
    if len(sel)==0: sel=[scored[0]]
    # If best_a not represented? maybe add best_a interp if its score better than first? It should be in inits.
    weights=(0.68, 0.20, 0.08, 0.04)
    stage_time_max = max(0.0, stage_deadline - time.monotonic())
    for rank in range(min(4, len(sel))):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now + 15 >= stage_deadline: break
       wgt=weights[rank] if rank < len(weights) else 0.02
       alloc = min(wgt*stage_time_max, stage_deadline - now - 8.0)
       if alloc < 10.0:
           alloc = max(5.0, stage_deadline - now - 8.0)
       if alloc <= 0: break
       seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31-1))
       a_init=sel[rank][1]
       a_opt, sc_opt = _local_search(a_init, n, float(alloc), seed)
       if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_score:
           best_a=_normalize_nonneg(a_opt); best_score=float(sc_opt)
           print("  n=%d rank=%d score=%.8f" % (n,rank,best_score))
    # final polish
    now=time.monotonic()
    if now + 40 < stage_deadline:
       init = _interp(best_a,n) if best_a is not None else sel[0][1]
       seed=int(rng.integers(0,2**31-1))
       a_opt, sc_opt = _local_search(init, n, float(max(10.0, stage_deadline-now-5.0)), seed)
       if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_score:
           best_a=_normalize_nonneg(a_opt); best_score=float(sc_opt)
    return best_a, float(best_score)
```
`stage_time_max` computed before candidates; if first candidate takes longer than alloc? `_local_search` bounded by seconds. Good. If first finishes early, stage_time_max remains original, so second alloc maybe larger than remaining? It caps `stage_deadline - now -8`. Good.

Now, `_make_inits` with m optional. Need if best_a passed for spectral uses m. Let's write.

```
def _make_inits(n, rng, best_a, m=None):
   out=[]
   n=int(n)
   if best_a is not None:
       ba=_interp(best_a,n)
       out.append(ba)
   else:
       ba=None
   if m is None: m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   out.append(np.full(n,1.0/n))
   # cos pi
   for eps in (0.3,0.55,0.8):
      for sh in (0.0,0.3,0.65):
          a=1.0 + eps*np.cos(np.pi*(t-sh))
          out.append(_normalize_nonneg(a))
   for p in (0.4,0.8,1.5):
      base=np.maximum(t*(1.0-t),1e-8)
      out.append(_normalize_nonneg(base**p))
      base2=0.5+0.5*np.cos(np.pi*t)
      out.append(_normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(base2,1e-8)**p))
   # gaussians
   for _ in range(4):
      k=int(rng.integers(2,6))
      centers=rng.uniform(0.05,0.95,k)
      sigmas=rng.uniform(0.04,0.25,k)
      amps=rng.uniform(0.6,1.6,k)
      a=np.zeros(n)
      for c,s,amp in zip(centers,sigmas,amps):
          a += amp*np.exp(-((t-c)**2)/(2*s*s))
      out.append(_normalize_nonneg(a))
   # fourier fixed t
   for K,amp0,use_pi,base in ((8,0.5,False,0.9),(16,0.45,True,1.0),(32,0.4,False,0.9),(24,0.5,True,1.1)):
      a=np.full(n,base)
      amp=amp0
      for k in range(1,K+1):
         if amp < 0.02: break
         ph=rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi)
         if use_pi:
            a += amp*np.cos(k*np.pi*t + ph)
         else:
            a += amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*k*t + ph)
         amp *= rng.uniform(0.5,0.75)
      out.append(_normalize_nonneg(a))
   # spectral from best
   if ba is not None:
      _, b_ba = _score_b(ba,m,n)
      L=2*n-1
      flat=np.full(L,1.0/float(L))
      if b_ba is not None and float(b_ba.sum())>0:
          b_norm=b_ba/b_ba.sum()
          mixes=(0.5,0.75,1.0)
          iters=18 if n>4096 else 25
          for mix in mixes:
              tgt=mix*b_norm + (1.0-mix)*flat
              a=_spectral_target_init(tgt, ba, n, m, iters, 0.5)
              out.append(a)
      # flat spectral
      a=_spectral_target_init(flat, ba, n, m, iters, 0.5)
      out.append(a)
   # limit maybe 30
   return out[:35]
```

Let's write `_spectral_target_init`:
```
def _spectral_target_init(target, a_start, n, m, iters, blend):
    L=2*n-1
    target=np.asarray(target,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if target.size < L:
       pad=np.zeros(L); pad[:target.size]=target; target=pad
    else: target=target[:L].copy()
    target=np.maximum(target,0.0)
    tm=float(target.max())
    if tm>0 and np.isfinite(tm): target=target/tm
    else: target=np.ones(L)
    T=np.fft.fft(target,m)
    a=_normalize_nonneg(a_start)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
    iters=max(1,int(iters))
    for it in range(iters):
        F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
        fabmax=float(np.max(np.abs(F)))
        if not np.isfinite(fabmax) or fabmax<=0: fabmax=1.0
        eps=1e-10*fabmax
        Fnew=0.5*(F + T/(F+eps))
        anew=np.fft.ifft(Fnew).real[:n]
        pos=np.maximum(anew,0.0)
        ps=float(pos.sum())
        asum=float(a.sum())
        if ps < 1e-6*max(asum,1e-12):
            pos=a.copy()
        bld=max(0.2, blend*(1.0 - 0.5*float(it)/float(iters)))
        a=(1.0-bld)*a + bld*pos
        a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
        amax=float(a.max())
        if amax>1e12: a=a*(1e12/amax)
    return _normalize_nonneg(a)
```
Potential issue: T may be complex, F+eps (complex scalar? eps real) okay. If Fnew has poor finite, continue. Add if not all finite break.

This spectral init might produce good but maybe not. Fine.

import operator
def _select_diverse(scored, max_keep, thresh):
    if not scored: return []
    order=list(range(len(scored)))
    order.sort(key=lambda? no. Use itemgetter? Need sort by score of scored[i]. itemgetter works on tuple? We can create `keys = [scored[i][0] for i in order]`? Simpler: create pairs `(score, index)` and sort.
    pairs=[(scored[i][0], i) for i in range(len(scored))]
    pairs.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    out=[]; used=[]
    for sc,i in pairs:
       if not np.isfinite(sc): continue
       a=scored[i][1]
       diverse=True
       for j in used:
           b=scored[j][1]
           d=np.sqrt(np.dot(a-b,a-b))
           if d < thresh: diverse=False; break
       if diverse:
           out.append((sc,a)); used.append(i)
           if len(out)>=max_keep: break
    if len(out)<max_keep:
       existing=set(used)
       for sc,i in pairs:
          if i in existing: continue
          out.append((scored[i][0], scored[i][1]))
          if len(out)>=max_keep: break
    return out

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import operator

"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing

    2 * n * max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The implementation uses:
  - interpolated previous best when available,
  - a broad set of analytic / random smooth initializations,
  - spectral-target factorization initializations,
  - a long running Adam-style optimizer in log-softmax coordinates,
  - annealed soft-max / top-lag / variance-weighted active-set gradients,
  - backtracking on the true max-convolution objective with an LSE
    escape clause,
  - progressive lengths (512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192) and a final polish.
"""


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _interp(a, n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    ss = float(out.sum())
    if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return out / ss


def _init_log(a):
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    v = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    v = v - float(v.mean())
    return np.clip(v, -30.0, 30.0)


def _center_clip(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v -= float(v.mean())
    np.clip(v, -30.0, 30.0, out=v)
    return v


def _softmax(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = v - float(v.max())
    e = np.exp(v)
    s = float(e.sum())
    n = v.size
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _score_b(a, m, n):
    """Return (score, b) for normalized a, using precomputed FFT length m."""
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = int(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
    b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    bsum = float(b.sum())
    bmax = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    if bsum <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(bmax) or bmax <= 0.0:
        return np.inf, b
    score = float(2.0 * n * bmax / (bsum * bsum))
    return score, b


def _eval_candidate(a, m, n, tau):
    """Score and average LSE of conv(a,a) for a normalized candidate."""
    n = int(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
    b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    bsum = float(b.sum())
    bmax = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    if bsum <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(bmax):
        return np.inf, np.inf
    score = float(2.0 * n * bmax / (bsum * bsum))
    if tau <= 1e-30:
        return score, bmax
    x = (b - bmax) / tau
    x -= float(x.max())
    x = np.clip(x, -60.0, 0.0)
    s = float(np.exp(x).sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        lse = bmax
    else:
        lse = bmax + float(np.log(s / float(b.size)))
    return score, lse


def _spectral_target_init(target, a_start, n, m, iters, blend):
    """Approximate square-factorization of a target convolution profile."""
    n = int(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    target = np.asarray(target, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if target.size < L:
        pad = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        pad[:target.size] = target
        target = pad
    else:
        target = target[:L].copy()
    target = np.maximum(target, 0.0)
    tm = float(target.max()) if target.size else 0.0
    if tm <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(tm):
        target = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        target = target / tm

    T = np.fft.fft(target, m)
    tnorm = float(np.max(np.abs(T)))
    if not np.isfinite(tnorm) or tnorm <= 0.0:
        tnorm = 1.0

    a = _normalize_nonneg(a_start)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    iters = int(max(1, iters))
    blend = float(blend)

    for it in range(iters):
        F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(F)):
            break
        fabmax = float(np.max(np.abs(F)))
        if not np.isfinite(fabmax) or fabmax <= 0.0:
            fabmax = 1.0
        eps = 1e-8 * max(1.0, fabmax, tnorm)
        Fnew = 0.5 * (F + T / (F + eps))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(Fnew)):
            break
        anew = np.fft.ifft(Fnew).real[:n]
        pos = np.maximum(anew, 0.0)
        ps = float(pos.sum())
        asum = float(a.sum())
        if ps < 1e-6 * max(asum, 1e-12):
            pos = a.copy()
        bld = max(0.20, blend * (1.0 - 0.5 * float(it) / float(iters)))
        a = (1.0 - bld) * a + bld * pos
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
        amax = float(a.max())
        if amax > 1e12:
            a = a * (1e12 / amax)
        if amax <= 0.0:
            break

    return _normalize_nonneg(a)


def _make_inits(n, rng, best_a, m=None):
    """Create many diverse normalized non-negative starting points."""
    n = int(n)
    out = []

    if best_a is not None:
        ba = _interp(best_a, n)
        out.append(ba)
    else:
        ba = None

    if m is None:
        m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    out.append(np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64))

    for eps in (0.30, 0.55, 0.80):
        for sh in (0.0, 0.30, 0.65):
            a = 1.0 + eps * np.cos(np.pi * (t - sh))
            out.append(_normalize_nonneg(a))

    for p in (0.40, 0.80, 1.50):
        base1 = np.maximum(t * (1.0 - t), 1e-8)
        out.append(_normalize_nonneg(base1 ** p))
        base2 = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * t)
        out.append(_normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(base2, 1e-8) ** p))

    for _ in range(4):
        k = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
        centers = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.95, k)
        sigmas = rng.uniform(0.04, 0.25, k)
        amps = rng.uniform(0.60, 1.60, k)
        a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for c, s, amp in zip(centers, sigmas, amps):
            a += amp * np.exp(-((t - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * s * s))
        out.append(_normalize_nonneg(a))

    fourier_specs = (
        (8, 0.50, False, 0.90),
        (16, 0.45, True, 1.00),
        (32, 0.40, False, 0.90),
        (24, 0.50, True, 1.10),
    )
    for K, amp0, use_pi, base in fourier_specs:
        a = np.full(n, float(base), dtype=np.float64)
        amp = float(amp0)
        for k in range(1, int(K) + 1):
            if amp < 0.02:
                break
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            if use_pi:
                a += amp * np.cos(k * np.pi * t + ph)
            else:
                a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph)
            amp *= float(rng.uniform(0.50, 0.75))
        out.append(_normalize_nonneg(a))

    if ba is not None:
        L = 2 * n - 1
        _, b_ba = _score_b(ba, m, n)
        flat = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        pos = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L, dtype=np.float64)
        edge = np.abs(pos - 0.5) * 2.0
        edge_target = 1.0 + 0.75 * (edge ** 1.5)

        b_norm = None
        if b_ba is not None and float(b_ba.sum()) > 0.0:
            bsum = float(b_ba.sum())
            b_norm = b_ba / bsum

        spectral_targets = [flat]
        if b_norm is not None:
            spectral_targets.append(b_norm)
            spectral_targets.append(0.55 * b_norm + 0.45 * flat)
            spectral_targets.append(0.50 * b_norm + 0.50 * edge_target)
        else:
            spectral_targets.append(edge_target)

        iters = 18 if n > 4096 else 26
        for tgt in spectral_targets:
            a_sp = _spectral_target_init(tgt, ba, n, m, iters, 0.50)
            out.append(_normalize_nonneg(a_sp))

    return out[:38]


def _select_diverse(scored, max_keep, thresh):
    """Pick top-scored but sufficiently distinct sequences."""
    if not scored:
        return []
    pairs = [(scored[i][0], i) for i in range(len(scored))]
    pairs.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))

    out = []
    used = []
    for sc, i in pairs:
        a = scored[i][1]
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        diverse = True
        for j in used:
            b = scored[j][1]
            d = float(np.sqrt(np.dot(a - b, a - b)))
            if d < thresh:
                diverse = False
                break
        if diverse:
            out.append((sc, a))
            used.append(i)
            if len(out) >= max_keep:
                break

    if len(out) < max_keep:
        used_set = set(used)
        for sc, i in pairs:
            if i in used_set:
                continue
            if not np.isfinite(scored[i][0]):
                continue
            out.append((scored[i][0], scored[i][1]))
            if len(out) >= max_keep:
                break

    return out


def _local_search(a0, n, seconds, seed, verbose=False):
    """Adam/log-softmax local optimizer with annealed max/flatness weights."""
    n = int(n)
    seconds = float(max(0.0, seconds))
    if seconds <= 0.0:
        a = _normalize_nonneg(a0)
        m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        sc, _ = _score_b(a, m, n)
        return a, sc

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    L = 2 * n - 1
    m = _next_pow2(L)
    factor = 2.0 * float(n)

    a = _normalize_nonneg(a0)
    v = _init_log(a)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_v = v.copy()
    best_sc, _ = _score_b(a, m, n)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = np.inf

    cur_v = v.copy()
    mu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    nu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lr = 0.35
    fail = 0

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, seconds)
    last_best = start

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        now = time.monotonic()
        prog = min(1.0, max(0.0, (now - start) / max(1.0, seconds)))

        stuck_limit = min(90.0, 0.22 * seconds + 18.0)
        if now - last_best > stuck_limit:
            sigma = 0.12 + 0.45 * (1.0 - prog)
            cur_v = best_v + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            cur_v = _center_clip(cur_v)
            lr = 0.25
            fail = 0
            last_best = now
            continue

        a = _softmax(cur_v)
        F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
        b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        bsum = float(b.sum())
        bmax = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
        if bsum <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(bmax):
            break
        sc = float(factor * bmax / (bsum * bsum))
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            break

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_v = cur_v.copy()
            last_best = now
            fail = 0

        # If the iterate drifts far above the run's own best, snap back.
        if sc > best_sc + 0.01:
            cur_v = best_v.copy()
            continue

        M = bmax

        tau_frac = 0.40 * (0.02 / 0.40) ** prog
        tau_frac *= float(rng.uniform(0.75, 1.30))
        tau = max(1e-15, tau_frac * M)

        top_frac = 0.05 * (0.003 / 0.05) ** prog
        top_frac = max(1.0 / float(L), min(0.15, top_frac))

        rho_top = 0.10 + 0.60 * prog
        alpha_sq = 0.35 * (1.0 - prog) + 0.05

        x = (b - M) / tau
        x -= float(x.max())
        x = np.clip(x, -60.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(x)
        sw = float(e.sum())
        if sw > 0.0 and np.isfinite(sw):
            wmax = e / sw
        else:
            wmax = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)

        K = int(round(L * top_frac))
        K = max(1, min(L, K))
        if K >= L:
            idx = np.arange(L)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
        wtop = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        wtop[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)

        if bsum > 0.0:
            wsq = b / bsum
        else:
            wsq = wmax

        signed_prob = 0.35 if prog < 0.40 else (0.15 if prog < 0.80 else 0.0)
        if rng.random() < signed_prob:
            mean = bsum / float(L)
            wv = b - mean
            sv = float(np.sum(np.abs(wv)))
            if sv > 0.0:
                wv = wv / sv
            w = 0.50 * wmax + 0.20 * wtop + 0.20 * wsq + 0.10 * wv
        else:
            base = 1.0 - rho_top - alpha_sq
            if base < 0.0:
                base = 0.0
            w = base * wmax + rho_top * wtop + alpha_sq * wsq
            wsum = float(w.sum())
            if wsum > 0.0 and np.isfinite(wsum):
                w = w / wsum

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
            w = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)

        P = np.fft.fft(w, m)
        c = np.fft.ifft(P * np.conj(F)).real[:n]
        G = a * (c - float(np.dot(c, a)))
        G = G - float(G.mean())
        gn = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(G * G)))

        if not np.isfinite(gn) or gn < 1e-14:
            cur_v = cur_v + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, n)
            cur_v = _center_clip(cur_v)
            continue

        t += 1
        mu = 0.90 * mu + 0.10 * G
        nu = 0.999 * nu + 0.001 * (G * G)
        mhat = mu / (1.0 - 0.90 ** t)
        vhat = nu / (1.0 - 0.999 ** t)
        step = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
        step = np.clip(step, -1.0, 1.0)
        step = step - float(step.mean())
        srms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(step * step)))
        if not np.isfinite(srms) or srms <= 1e-14:
            continue

        lse_cur = M + float(np.log(sw / float(L))) if sw > 0.0 else M

        best_cand_v = None
        best_cand_sc = sc
        best_cand_lse = lse_cur
        accepted = False
        accepted_v = None
        accepted_sc = sc

        for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            v_try = cur_v - lr * fac * step
            v_try = _center_clip(v_try)
            a_try = _softmax(v_try)
            sc2, lse2 = _eval_candidate(a_try, m, n, tau)
            if not np.isfinite(sc2):
                continue

            if (
                sc2 < best_cand_sc - 1e-12
                or (abs(sc2 - best_cand_sc) <= 1e-12 and lse2 < best_cand_lse - 1e-12)
            ):
                best_cand_sc = sc2
                best_cand_lse = lse2
                best_cand_v = v_try.copy()

            if sc2 < sc - 1e-10:
                accepted = True
                accepted_v = v_try
                accepted_sc = sc2
                break

        if not accepted and best_cand_v is not None:
            raw_tol = max(1e-8, 1e-6 * best_sc)
            if best_cand_sc < sc + raw_tol and best_cand_lse < lse_cur - 1e-6:
                accepted = True
                accepted_v = best_cand_v
                accepted_sc = best_cand_sc

        if accepted:
            cur_v = accepted_v
            if accepted_sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
                best_sc = float(accepted_sc)
                best_v = accepted_v.copy()
                best_a = _softmax(accepted_v)
                last_best = now
            fail = 0
            if accepted_sc < sc:
                lr = min(1.2, lr * 1.04)
            else:
                lr = max(0.01, lr * 0.98)
        else:
            fail += 1
            lr = max(0.005, lr * 0.85)
            if fail > 60:
                sigma = 0.10 + 0.28 * (1.0 - prog)
                cur_v = best_v + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                cur_v = _center_clip(cur_v)
                lr = 0.20
                fail = 0

    a_out = _softmax(best_v)
    a_out = _normalize_nonneg(a_out)
    sc_out, _ = _score_b(a_out, m, n)
    if not np.isfinite(sc_out) or sc_out > best_sc:
        a_out = best_a
        sc_out = best_sc
    return a_out, float(sc_out)


def _process_stage(n, stage_deadline, best_a, best_score, rng):
    now = time.monotonic()
    n = int(n)
    if now >= stage_deadline or n < 3:
        return best_a, best_score

    L = 2 * n - 1
    m = _next_pow2(L)

    if stage_deadline - now < 20.0:
        init = _interp(best_a, n) if best_a is not None else np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        sc, _ = _score_b(init, m, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            return _normalize_nonneg(init), float(sc)
        return best_a, best_score

    inits = _make_inits(n, rng, best_a, m)
    scored = []
    for a in inits:
        a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
        sc, _ = _score_b(a, m, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, a))

    if not scored:
        init = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        sc, _ = _score_b(init, m, n)
        scored = [(sc, init)]

    sel = _select_diverse(scored, 6, 0.055)
    if not sel:
        sel = [scored[0]]

    weights = (0.68, 0.20, 0.08, 0.04)
    stage_time_max = max(0.0, stage_deadline - time.monotonic())

    for rank in range(min(4, len(sel))):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 15.0 >= stage_deadline:
            break
        wgt = weights[rank] if rank < len(weights) else 0.02
        alloc = min(wgt * stage_time_max, stage_deadline - now - 8.0)
        if alloc < 10.0:
            alloc = max(5.0, stage_deadline - now - 8.0)
        if alloc <= 0.0:
            break
        seed = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
        a_opt, sc_opt = _local_search(sel[rank][1], n, float(alloc), seed)
        if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_score:
            best_a = _normalize_nonneg(a_opt)
            best_score = float(sc_opt)
            print("    stage n=%d rank=%d score=%.8f" % (n, rank, best_score))

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now + 40.0 < stage_deadline:
        init = _interp(best_a, n) if best_a is not None else sel[0][1]
        seed = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
        a_opt, sc_opt = _local_search(init, n, float(max(10.0, stage_deadline - now - 5.0)), seed)
        if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_score:
            best_a = _normalize_nonneg(a_opt)
            best_score = float(sc_opt)

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(max(60.0, float(budget_s)))
    deadline = start + max(30.0, budget - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    prev = None
    try:
        cand = height_sequence_1
        arr = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            if float(arr.sum()) > 0.0:
                prev = arr / float(arr.sum())
    except Exception:
        prev = None

    if prev is not None:
        best_a = prev
        m = _next_pow2(2 * len(prev) - 1)
        best_score, _ = _score_b(best_a, m, len(best_a))
    else:
        best_a = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
        m = _next_pow2(2 * 512 - 1)
        best_score, _ = _score_b(best_a, m, len(best_a))

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
        m = _next_pow2(2 * 512 - 1)
        best_score, _ = _score_b(best_a, m, len(best_a))

    print("initial best score = %.8f, len=%d" % (best_score, len(best_a)))

    total_budget = max(60.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    stages = [
        (512, 0.02),
        (1024, 0.05),
        (2048, 0.45),
        (4096, 0.35),
        (8192, 0.08),
    ]

    for n, frac in stages:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 30.0 >= deadline:
            break
        stage_time = total_budget * float(frac)
        if stage_time < 20.0:
            stage_time = 20.0
        stage_deadline = min(deadline - 5.0, now + stage_time)
        if stage_deadline <= now:
            continue
        print("stage n=%d budget=%.1fs current_best=%.8f" % (n, stage_time, best_score))
        best_a, best_score = _process_stage(int(n), stage_deadline, best_a, best_score, rng)
        print("after stage n=%d best=%.8f len=%d" % (n, best_score, len(best_a)))

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now + 120.0 < deadline:
        n_extra = len(best_a) if best_a is not None else 2048
        if n_extra < 2048:
            n_extra = min(4096, max(1024, 2 * n_extra))
        extra_deadline = min(deadline - 5.0, now + max(60.0, (deadline - now) * 0.75))
        print("extra polish n=%d budget=%.1fs current_best=%.8f" % (
            n_extra, max(0.0, extra_deadline - now), best_score))
        best_a, best_score = _process_stage(int(n_extra), extra_deadline, best_a, best_score, rng)
        print("after extra polish best=%.8f len=%d" % (best_score, len(best_a)))

    best_a = _normalize_nonneg(best_a)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0 and len(best_a) <= 8192:
        try:
            exact = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a])
            print("final FFT score = %.8f, exact score = %.8f, len=%d" % (
                best_score, exact, len(best_a)))
            if not np.isfinite(exact):
                best_a = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
                best_score, _ = _score_b(best_a, _next_pow2(2 * 512 - 1), 512)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
